def fft_grad_weighted(a, w):
    n=len(a); L=2*n-1; size=1 << (3*n-2).bit_length()

```

def gen_power(n,a,b,eps=0.5,shift=1):
  x=np.arange(n)+eps  # 0.5..n-0.5
  t=x/n
  w=np.power(x, -a) * np.power(max(1-t,1e-12), b)
  w=np.maximum(w,0)
  return w
```

Implement `polish_adam_z(a_init, max_time, rng, beta_init=1, beta_max=2000, lr0=0.02)`:
```
S=sum(a); a=norm; minp=1e-12*S/n? too small.
z=np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-6*S/n))
z -= np.max(z)
m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); b1=0.9,b2=0.99
lr=lr0
best=a; best_sc=score; plateau=0
for it in range(max possible until deadline):
    beta = min(beta_max, beta_init*(1+0.005*it))
    c,sc,grad_a = conv_and_grad(a,beta)
    if sc < best_sc: best=a copy; plateau=0 else plateau++
    # compute grad_z
    mean = np.mean(a*grad_a) ? p*grad, p=a/S, mean=np.sum(a*grad_a)/S
    grad_z = a * (grad_a - mean)
    # if grad_z finite
    m = b1*m + (1-b1)*grad_z
    v = b2*v + (1-b2)*grad_z*grad_z
    mhat=m/(1-b1**it); vhat=v/(1-b2**it)
    z = z + lr*mhat/(np.sqrt(vhat)+1e-8)
    # recompute a
    zm = z - np.max(z); exp=np.exp(zm); a = S*exp/sum
    a = np.maximum(a,0)
    # zero very small? a[a < 1e-10*S/n]=0; normalize
    # optional accept? We don't roll back. But if z update gives NaN, revert.
    if plateau>2000: # perturb
        a = normalize(a + rng.normal(0, 0.01*S/n, n), S)
        z=log(a+eps); m=v=0; lr=0.01
```

State can be a Python list or dict: `state = {'a':None,'score':inf}`.
Top-level:
```
def consider_candidate(a, state, label, print_log=True):
   a=np.asarray...
   a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
   s=a.sum()
   if s<=0.01: return
   n=len(a); S=np.sqrt(2*n)
   a=a*S/s
   sc=score_sequence(a)
   if np.isfinite(sc) and sc<state['score']:
       state['score']=sc; state['a']=a.copy(); state['n']=n; state['label']=label
       print(...)
```

Optimize_subgrad:
```
def optimize_subgrad(a_init, state, deadline, rng, max_time):
  n; S; a=normalize; current sc=score; end_time=start+max_time
  step_factor=1.0; no_improve=0; top_k=2
  for it:
   if time>end break
   c,sc Exact from score_sequence? We need c for active; use conv_self only (no LSE). cost 1 forward+inverse.
   active = np.argpartition(c, -top_k)[-top_k:] if top_k<n else all
   vals=c[active]; weights=vals-vals.min()+1e-12; weights/=sum
   fill w_full
   grad=grad_weighted(a,w_full)
   gnorm2=dot(grad,grad)
   maxc=c.max()
   alpha=2*maxc/(gnorm2+eps)
   # candidates multipliers [0.25,0.5,1,2]
   best_scand=sc; best_cand=a
   for mult:
      cand=project_simplex(a - mult*alpha*grad,S)
      scand=score_sequence(cand)
      if scand<best_scand: best_scand=scand; best_cand=cand
   if best_scand < sc* (1 - 1e-9): update a, state, no_improve=0
   else: no_improve++
   if no_improve==50:
       # perturbation
       a=normalize(a + rng.normal(0,0.005*S/np.sqrt(n),n),S)
       no_improve=0
       top_k = min(16, top_k+1)
   top_k = min(16, 2 + it//500)
```
Cost: score current + grad + up to4 score candidates =6 FFT, similar. Fewer iterations.

Define:
```
def gen_power_shape(n, a_exp, b_exp):
    t=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
    w=np.ones(n)
    if a_exp>0: w *= np.power(np.maximum(t,1e-8), -a_exp)
    if b_exp!=0:
       u=np.maximum(1-t,1e-8)
       if b_exp>0: w *= np.power(u,b_exp)
       else: w *= np.power(u,b_exp) # negative gives large
    return w
```
For b negative, w at last t=1-0.5/n -> u~0.5/n, power negative huge ~n^0.5, sum maybe dominated; ok. But can create values large but normalize.
`gen_exp_shape(n, lam, p=1)`: `np.exp(-lam*t**p)`.
`gen_poly_shape(n, coeffs)`: `p=1 + coeffs[0]*t + coeffs[1]*t*t + coeffs[2]*t**3 + coeffs[3]*t**4`? Clip.
`gen_trig_shape(n, amps, phases)`: frequencies `[1,2,3,4]`; p=1 + sum a_k sin(2π k t + φ_k). To avoid all negative, after generation `p=np.maximum(p,1e-6)`.

def consider_candidate(a, pool, state, label):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size<64: return
    arr = np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
    s=arr.sum()

    n=arr.size; S=np.sqrt(2.0*n)

```
w = np.sort(v)[::-1]
cs = np.cumsum(w)
rho = np.arange(1,n+1)
cond = (cs - S)/rho <= w
rho_idx = np.max(nonzero(cond))
theta = (cs[rho_idx-1]-S)/rho_idx
return np.maximum(v-theta,0)
```

```
"""Docstring"""
import numpy as np, time, math
from scipy.fft import fft, ifft

TARGET = 1.5027

def pool_key(item): return item[0]

def propose_candidate():

    # add top pool excluding duplicates labels? We can just add top 4.

       if len(starts)>=5: break
       # skip if label already? not necessary.

       # run subgrad with remaining/2, adam remaining/2

Let's implement `softmax_normalize(z, S)` top-level:
```
def softmax_normalize(z,S):
    z = np.asarray(z,float)
    z = z - np.max(z)
    z = np.clip(z, -700, 700)
    p = np.exp(z)
    s=p.sum()
    if s <=0: return np.full(len(z), S/len(z))
    return S*p/s
```
In Adam, z update can grow; clipping helps.

`optimize_adam_z` detailed:

def optimize_adam_z(a_init, state, deadline, rng, end_time):
    n=len(a_init); S=np.sqrt(2*n)
    a=normalize_sum(np.asarray(a_init),S)
    sc=score_sequence(a)
    update_state(a,sc,state,'adam_init')
    m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=1
    base=S/n
    floor=1e-7*base if base>0 else 1e-12
    pos=np.maximum(a,floor)
    z=np.log(pos)
    z = z - np.max(z)
    lr0=0.02
    beta_init=1.0; beta_max=2000.0
    reject_count=0
    it=0
    start=time.time? use monotonic
    while time.monotonic()<end_time and time.monotonic()<deadline:
        it+=1
        beta=min(beta_max, beta_init*(1.0+0.003*it))
        c,sc,grad_a = conv_and_grad(a,beta)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < state['score']-1e-12: update_state(a,sc,state,'adam')
        # if current score too bad, maybe revert? not.
        mean = np.sum(a*grad_a)/S
        grad_z = a*(grad_a - mean)
        # if grad_z tiny break
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_z)):
            grad_z = np.nan_to_num(grad_z,0)
        m = 0.9*m +0.1*grad_z
        v = 0.99*v +0.01*grad_z*grad_z
        bm = m/(1-0.9**it)
        bv = v/(1-0.99**it)
        lr = lr0 / np.sqrt(1.0+0.01*it)
        z_new = z + lr * bm/(np.sqrt(bv)+1e-8)
        z_new = z_new - np.max(z_new)
        a_new = softmax_normalize(z_new,S)
        # zero tiny: a_new[a_new < 1e-10*base]=0; if sum? keep sum
        sc_new = score_sequence(a_new)
        if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < max(sc*1.1, state['score']*1.2):
             a=a_new; z=z_new; reject_count=0
             if sc_new < state['score']: update_state(a,sc_new,state,'adam')
        else:
             reject_count +=1
             if reject_count>20:
                 # shrink lr by reducing base and reset moment
                 lr0 *=0.7
                 # optionally perturb current a and z
                 a = normalize_sum(a + rng.normal(0, 0.01*base, n), S)
                 z=np.log(np.maximum(a, floor)); z=z-np.max(z)
                 m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n)
                 reject_count=0
             else:
                 z stays old; a stays old; but m,v updated? Could keep? We'll revert m/v? To avoid contaminated, if reject, reapply previous? We already updated m/v. Simpler before update save m_old? Not. Could set `m = m - 0.1*grad_z`? Not.
```

    t_new=...

    a=x
    if stagnate (state no improve for 1000): perturb y

`conv_and_grad`:
```
def conv_and_grad(a,beta):
    n=len(a); s=a.sum()
    if s<=0: return None,inf,zero
    L=2*n-1; size=1 << ((L-1).bit_length())
    A=fft(a,size)
    c=np.real(ifft(A*A))[:L]
    maxc=float(np.max(c)) if L else 0.0
    if maxc<0: maxc=0.0
    score=float(2.0*n*maxc/(s*s))
    if beta <=0:
       # active single max? not used
    else:
       exp_bc=np.exp(beta*(c-maxc))
       total=float(exp_bc.sum())
       if total <=0: w=zeros
       else w=exp_bc/total
    if float(w.sum()) <=0: w zeros
    W=fft(w,size)
    grad=2.0*np.real(ifft(np.conj(A)*W))[:n]
    return c,score,grad
```
If beta very high and only one c=max=1, w one-hot. Good.

`score_sequence`:
```
def score_sequence(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float); s=a.sum(); if s<0.01 return inf
   c=fft_convolve_self(a); maxc=float(c.max()); if maxc<0 maxc=0
   return 2*a.size*maxc/(s*s)
```

`normalize_sum`:
```
def normalize_sum(a,S):
   a=np.asarray(a,float); a=np.clip(a,0,1000); s=a.sum()
   if s<=0: return np.full(len(a), S/len(a))
   return a*(S/s)
```

def gen_random_shape(rng,n,kind):
   t=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
   if kind==0: # power
      a_exp=rng.uniform(0,0.5); b_exp=rng.uniform(-0.5,1.0)
      w=np.ones(n)
      if a_exp>0: w *= np.power(np.maximum(t,1e-8), -a_exp)
      u=np.maximum(1-t,1e-8)
      if b_exp>0: w *= np.power(u,b_exp)
      elif b_exp<0: w *= np.power(u,b_exp)
      if rng.random()<0.5: w=w[::-1]
   elif kind==1: exp
   ...
   return np.maximum(w,0)
```

Top-level functions:
```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.fft import fft, ifft

TARGET_SCORE = 1.5027

def pool_key(item):
    return item[0]

def next_fft_size(n_seq):
    L=2*n_seq-1
    return 1 << (L-1).bit_length()

def fft_convolve_self(a):
    n=len(a); size=next_fft_size(n); A=fft(a,size); return np.real(ifft(A*A))[:2*n-1]

def score_sequence(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size==0: return np.inf
    s=float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<0.01: return np.inf
    c=fft_convolve_self(a)
    m=float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m<0: m=0.0
    return float(2.0*a.size*m/(s*s))

def conv_and_grad(a,beta): ...
```

def update_state(a, sc, state, label):

    if not np.isfinite(sc): return
    if sc < state['score'] - 1e-12:
        state['score']=float(sc); state['a']=np.asarray(a,float).copy(); state['label']=label
        print('new best [%s] score=%.8f' % (label, sc))

```
def consider_candidate(a,pool,state,label):
   arr=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
   if arr.size<64: return
   arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
   s=arr.sum()
   if s<0.01 or not finite: return
   n=arr.size; S=np.sqrt(2*n)
   arr=arr*(S/s)
   sc=score_sequence(arr)
   if not finite: return
   update_state(arr, sc, state, label)
   if len(pool) < 200:
      pool.append((float(sc), arr.copy(), label))
   else:
      # if score better than worst, replace? Find max score
      worst_idx=0; worst=-1
      for idx,item in enumerate(pool):
         if item[0]>worst: worst=item[0]; worst_idx=idx
      if sc < worst:
         pool[worst_idx]=(float(sc), arr.copy(), label)
```

def optimize_fista(a_init,state,deadline,rng,end_time):
    n=len(a_init); S=np.sqrt(2.0*n)
    a=normalize_sum(a_init,S)
    sc=score_sequence(a); update_state(...)
    y=a.copy(); t=1.0; it=0
    no_imp=None? Use state_score_start=state['score']
    while time.monotonic()<end_time and time.monotonic()<deadline:
       it+=1
       beta=1.0*(1.0+0.002*it); if beta>500: beta=500
       c,sc_y,grad=conv_and_grad(y,beta)
       # y feasible? initially yes; after projection yes.
       gnorm=float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
       if gnorm<1e-14: break
       base=S/n
       step=1.0*base/ max(gnorm,1e-12)
       # cap step to avoid crazy: if step>10: step=10? base/gnorm if gnorm tiny huge.
       if not finite step or step>10: step=10
       x=project_simplex(y - step*grad, S)
       # every few, exact
       if it % 3 == 0 or x != y? just every 5:
           sc_x=score_sequence(x); update_state(...)
       t_new=0.5*(1+np.sqrt(1+4*t*t))
       y=x + ((t-1.0)/t_new)*(x-a)
       y=project_simplex(y,S)
       a=x; t=t_new
       # stagnation: if state.score not improved in 2000, perturb
       if it % 2000 == 0:
           y=project_simplex(y + rng.normal(0, 0.01*base, n), S)
           t=1.0
```

`optimize_subgrad`:

def optimize_subgrad(a_init,state,deadline,rng,end_time):
   n; S; a=normalize; sc=score; update
   it=0; no_improve=0; top_k=3
   while time<end_time and time<deadline:
      it+=1
      c,sc=conv_and_score(a)
      update_state(a,sc,state,'subgrad')
      maxc=float(np.max(c))
      if maxc<=0: break
      top_k=min(64, 3+it//200)
      if top_k >= L: idx=np.arange(L); vals=c
      else: idx=np.argpartition(c, -top_k)[-top_k:]
      vals=c[idx]
      w=vals - vals.min() + 1e-12; w/=w.sum()
      wf=np.zeros(L); wf[idx]=w
      grad=grad_weighted(a,wf)
      gnorm=np.linalg.norm(grad)
      if gnorm<1e-14: break
      base=S/n
      step=1.0*base/max(gnorm,1e-12)
      best_scand=sc; best_cand=a
      for mult in (0.05,0.1,0.25,0.5,1.0,2.0):
          cand=project_simplex(a - mult*step*grad, S)
          scand=score_sequence(cand)
          if np.isfinite(scand) and scand < best_scand:
              best_scand=scand; best_cand=cand
      if best_scand < sc - 1e-12:
          a=best_cand; update_state(a,best_scand,state,'subgrad'); no_improve=0
      else:
          no_improve+=1
          if no_improve > 50:
             # perturb or reset to best
             if state['a'] is not None and rng.random()<0.5:
                a=normalize_sum(state['a'],S)
             else:
                a=normalize_sum(a + rng.normal(0,0.02*base,n),S)
             no_improve=0
```

If starts include a small length candidate (e.g., random n_main? all candidates n_main; given length 8192; no other lengths). We didn't generate other n. So state length n_main mostly. Fallback if no: 1024. Optimizers use 1024. Good.

```python
"""
Search for non-negative sequences minimizing
    2 * n * max(convolve(a, a)) / sum(a)^2.

Strategy:
- Repeatedly construct diverse analytic initial profiles (powers, ramps,
  exponentials, smoothed splines, trigonometric/bump shapes).
- Refine promising profiles with several first-order optimizers:
  * FISTA-like projected gradient on a log-sum-exp surrogate.
  * Adam optimization in log-softmax coordinates with a smoothed max model.
  * Active-lag projected subgradient method with line search.
- Keep the best exact FFT-scored sequence and return it.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.fft import fft, ifft


TARGET_SCORE = 1.5027


def pool_key(item):
    return item[0]


def next_fft_size(n_seq):
    L = 2 * n_seq - 1
    return 1 << (L - 1).bit_length()


def fft_convolve_self(a):
    n = len(a)
    size = next_fft_size(n)
    A = fft(a, size)
    c = np.real(ifft(A * A))
    return c[:2 * n - 1]


def score_sequence(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        return np.inf
    c = fft_convolve_self(a)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * a.size * m / (s * s))


def conv_and_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        return np.inf, np.zeros(max(1, 2 * n - 1), dtype=np.float64)
    c = fft_convolve_self(a)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    score = float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))
    return score, c


def conv_and_grad(a, beta):
    """Exact max score plus gradient of a log-sum-exp surrogate."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        return (np.zeros(2 * n - 1), np.inf, np.zeros(n))
    L = 2 * n - 1
    size = next_fft_size(n)
    A = fft(a, size)
    c = np.real(ifft(A * A))[:L]
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    score = float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))

    if beta > 0.0:
        exp_bc = np.exp(beta * (c - m))
        total = float(exp_bc.sum())
        if total > 0.0:
            w = exp_bc / total
        else:
            w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
            w[0] = 1.0
    else:
        w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        imax = int(np.argmax(c))
        w[imax] = 1.0

    W = fft(w, size)
    grad = 2.0 * np.real(ifft(np.conj(A) * W))[:n]
    grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return c, score, grad


def grad_weighted(a, w):
    n = len(a)
    size = next_fft_size(n)
    A = fft(a, size)
    W = fft(w, size)
    grad = 2.0 * np.real(ifft(np.conj(A) * W))[:n]
    return np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)


def normalize_sum(a, S):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    a = np.nan_to_num(a, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(a.size, S / max(1, a.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return a * (S / s)


def project_simplex(v, S):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64)
    n = len(v)
    if n == 0:
        return v.copy()
    v = np.nan_to_num(v, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)
    if S <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    w = np.sort(v)[::-1]
    cum = np.cumsum(w)
    idx = np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    theta_cand = (cum - S) / idx
    valid = theta_cand <= w
    if not np.any(valid):
        rho = n
    else:
        rho = int(np.max(np.where(valid)[0]) + 1)
    theta = (cum[rho - 1] - S) / rho
    out = np.maximum(v - theta, 0.0)
    return out


def softmax_normalize(z, S):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=-700.0, posinf=700.0, neginf=-700.0)
    z = z - np.max(z)
    z = np.clip(z, -700.0, 700.0)
    p = np.exp(z)
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(z.size, S / max(1, z.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return S * p / s


def update_state(a, sc, state, label):
    if a is None:
        return
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return
    if sc < state["score"] - 1e-12:
        state["score"] = float(sc)
        state["a"] = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        state["label"] = label
        print("new best [%s] score=%.8f len=%d" % (label, sc, len(state["a"])))


def consider_candidate(a, pool, state, label):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size < 64:
        return
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        return
    n = arr.size
    S = np.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))
    arr = arr * (S / s)
    sc = score_sequence(arr)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return

    update_state(arr, sc, state, label)

    if len(pool) < 200:
        pool.append((float(sc), arr.copy(), label))
    else:
        worst_idx = 0
        worst_sc = -np.inf
        for j, item in enumerate(pool):
            if item[0] > worst_sc:
                worst_sc = item[0]
                worst_idx = j
        if sc < worst_sc:
            pool[worst_idx] = (float(sc), arr.copy(), label)


def gen_power_shape(n, a_exp, b_exp):
    t = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    w = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if a_exp > 0.0:
        w *= np.power(np.maximum(t, 1e-9), -a_exp)
    u = np.maximum(1.0 - t, 1e-9)
    if abs(b_exp) > 1e-12:
        w *= np.power(u, b_exp)
    w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
    return np.clip(w, 0.0, 1e6)


def gen_exp_shape(n, lam, pw):
    t = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)
    w = np.exp(-lam * np.power(t, pw))
    return np.clip(w, 0.0, 1e6)


def gen_random_shape(rng, n, kind):
    t = (np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n)

    if kind == 0:
        a_exp = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 0.5))
        b_exp = float(rng.uniform(-0.6, 1.2))
        w = gen_power_shape(n, a_exp, b_exp)
        if rng.random() < 0.5:
            w = w[::-1]
        return w

    if kind == 1:
        lam = float(rng.uniform(0.3, 6.0))
        pw = float(rng.choice((0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0)))
        w = gen_exp_shape(n, lam, pw)
        if rng.random() < 0.5:
            w = w[::-1]
        return w

    if kind == 2:
        c = rng.uniform(-1.5, 1.5, size=4)
        w = 1.0 + c[0] * t + c[1] * t * t + c[2] * t ** 3 + c[3] * np.exp(-1.5 * t)
        w = np.maximum(w, 1e-4)
        return w

    if kind == 3:
        freqs = (1.0, 2.0, 3.0, 4.0)
        amps = rng.normal(0.0, 0.65, size=len(freqs))
        phases = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi, size=len(freqs))
        w = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for f, amp, ph in zip(freqs, amps, phases):
            w += amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * t + ph)
        w = np.maximum(w, 1e-4)
        return w

    # kind 4: smooth bump(s)
    c1 = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.95))
    w1 = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.45))
    w = 0.1 + np.exp(-((t - c1) / w1) ** 2)
    if rng.random() < 0.65:
        c2 = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.95))
        w2 = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.45))
        w += 0.35 * np.exp(-((t - c2) / w2) ** 2)
    return w


def optimize_fista(a_init, state, deadline, rng, end_time):
    a_init = np.asarray(a_init, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a_init.size
    S = np.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))
    a = normalize_sum(a_init, S)
    sc = score_sequence(a)
    update_state(a, sc, state, "fista-init")

    y = a.copy()
    t = 1.0
    it = 0
    no_improve = 0

    while time.monotonic() < end_time and time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        beta = 1.0 * (1.0 + 0.002 * it)
        if beta > 500.0:
            beta = 500.0

        _, sc_y, grad = conv_and_grad(y, beta)
        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
        if not np.isfinite(gnorm) or gnorm < 1e-14:
            break

        base = S / float(n)
        step = 1.0 * base / max(gnorm, 1e-12)
        if not np.isfinite(step):
            break
        if step > 10.0:
            step = 10.0

        x = project_simplex(y - step * grad, S)

        if it % 5 == 0:
            sc_x = score_sequence(x)
            if np.isfinite(sc_x):
                before = state["score"]
                update_state(x, sc_x, state, "fista")
                if state["score"] < before - 1e-9:
                    no_improve = 0
                else:
                    no_improve += 1

            if no_improve > 400 and state.get("a") is not None and len(state["a"]) == n:
                a = normalize_sum(state["a"], S)
                y = a.copy()
                t = 1.0
                no_improve = 0

        t_new = 0.5 * (1.0 + np.sqrt(1.0 + 4.0 * t * t))
        y = x + ((t - 1.0) / t_new) * (x - a)
        y = project_simplex(y, S)
        a = x
        t = t_new

        if it % 3000 == 0:
            y = project_simplex(y + rng.normal(0.0, 0.01 * base, n), S)
            t = 1.0


def optimize_adam_z(a_init, state, deadline, rng, end_time):
    a_init = np.asarray(a_init, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a_init.size
    S = np.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))
    a = normalize_sum(a_init, S)
    sc = score_sequence(a)
    update_state(a, sc, state, "adam-init")

    base = S / float(n)
    floor = max(1e-12 * base, 1e-300)
    floor = max(floor, 1e-300)

    pos = np.maximum(a, floor)
    z = np.log(pos)
    z = z - np.max(z)

    m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b1 = 0.90
    b2 = 0.99
    lr0 = 0.025
    it = 0
    reject_count = 0

    while time.monotonic() < end_time and time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        beta = 1.0 * (1.0 + 0.003 * it)
        if beta > 3000.0:
            beta = 3000.0

        _, sc, grad_a = conv_and_grad(a, beta)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            before = state["score"]
            update_state(a, sc, state, "adam")
            if state["score"] < before - 1e-9:
                reject_count = 0

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_a)):
            grad_a = np.nan_to_num(grad_a, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)

        mean_grad = float(np.sum(a * grad_a) / S)
        grad_z = a * (grad_a - mean_grad)
        grad_z = np.nan_to_num(grad_z, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
        if float(np.max(np.abs(grad_z))) < 1e-16:
            break

        m = b1 * m + (1.0 - b1) * grad_z
        v = b2 * v + (1.0 - b2) * (grad_z * grad_z)

        bias1 = 1.0 - b1 ** it
        bias2 = 1.0 - b2 ** it
        m_hat = m / bias1
        v_hat = v / bias2

        lr = lr0 / np.sqrt(1.0 + 0.01 * it)
        step = lr * m_hat / (np.sqrt(v_hat) + 1e-8)
        step = np.nan_to_num(step, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)

        z_new = z + step
        z_new = z_new - np.max(z_new)
        z_new = np.clip(z_new, -700.0, 700.0)
        a_new = softmax_normalize(z_new, S)
        a_new[a_new < 1e-10 * base] = 0.0
        s_new = float(a_new.sum())
        if s_new > 0.0:
            a_new *= (S / s_new)

        sc_new = score_sequence(a_new)
        if state.get("a") is not None:
            accept_threshold = max(sc * 1.02, state["score"] * 1.05)
        else:
            accept_threshold = sc * 1.02

        if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new <= accept_threshold:
            z = z_new
            a = a_new
            reject_count = 0
            update_state(a, sc_new, state, "adam")
        else:
            reject_count += 1
            if reject_count > 60:
                if state.get("a") is not None and len(state["a"]) == n and rng.random() < 0.6:
                    a = normalize_sum(state["a"], S)
                    sc = score_sequence(a)
                    update_state(a, sc, state, "adam-reset-best")
                else:
                    a = normalize_sum(a + rng.normal(0.0, 0.02 * base, n), S)
                    sc = score_sequence(a)
                    update_state(a, sc, state, "adam-reset-perturb")
                z = np.log(np.maximum(a, floor))
                z = z - np.max(z)
                m = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
                lr0 = max(0.003, lr0 * 0.6)
                reject_count = 0


def optimize_subgrad(a_init, state, deadline, rng, end_time):
    a_init = np.asarray(a_init, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a_init.size
    L = 2 * n - 1
    S = np.sqrt(2.0 * float(n))
    a = normalize_sum(a_init, S)
    sc, c = conv_and_score(a)
    update_state(a, sc, state, "subgrad-init")

    it = 0
    no_improve = 0
    base = S / float(n)

    while time.monotonic() < end_time and time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        sc, c = conv_and_score(a)
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            break

        update_state(a, sc, state, "subgrad")

        maxc = float(np.max(c))
        if maxc <= 0.0:
            break

        top_k = min(64, 3 + it // 200)
        if top_k >= L:
            idx = np.arange(L)
        else:
            idx = np.argpartition(c, -top_k)[-top_k:]

        vals = c[idx]
        weights = vals - float(np.min(vals)) + 1e-12
        weights = weights / weights.sum()

        w_full = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        w_full[idx] = weights
        grad = grad_weighted(a, w_full)
        gnorm = float(np.linalg.norm(grad))
        if not np.isfinite(gnorm) or gnorm < 1e-14:
            break

        step = 1.0 * base / max(gnorm, 1e-12)
        if not np.isfinite(step):
            break
        if step > 50.0:
            step = 50.0

        best_scand = sc
        best_cand = a
        for mult in (0.05, 0.1, 0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
            cand = project_simplex(a - (mult * step) * grad, S)
            scand = score_sequence(cand)
            if np.isfinite(scand) and scand < best_scand:
                best_scand = scand
                best_cand = cand

        if best_scand < sc - 1e-12:
            a = best_cand
            no_improve = 0
            update_state(a, best_scand, state, "subgrad")
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 60:
                if state.get("a") is not None and len(state["a"]) == n and rng.random() < 0.5:
                    a = normalize_sum(state["a"], S)
                else:
                    a = normalize_sum(a + rng.normal(0.0, 0.02 * base, n), S)
                no_improve = 0


def propose_candidate():
    start_time = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start_time + 2380.0

    rng = np.random.default_rng(20260101)
    n_main = 8192

    pool = []
    state = {"score": np.inf, "a": None, "label": "none"}

    # Reuse any previous SOTA sequence if present.
    given = None
    try:
        raw = height_sequence_1
        given = np.asarray(raw, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if given.size >= 64:
            given = np.clip(given, 0.0, 1000.0)
            given = np.nan_to_num(given, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
        else:
            given = None
    except Exception:
        given = None

    if given is not None:
        consider_candidate(given, pool, state, "given")
        if given.size == n_main:
            consider_candidate(given[::-1], pool, state, "given-rev")

            blend_ri = given[::-1].copy()
            for alpha in (0.2, 0.35, 0.5, 0.65, 0.8):
                consider_candidate(alpha * given + (1.0 - alpha) * blend_ri,
                                   pool, state, "mix-rev-%d" % int(100 * alpha))

            for win in (3, 5, 8, 13, 21, 33):
                kern = np.ones(win, dtype=np.float64) / float(win)
                blurred = np.convolve(given, kern, mode="same")
                consider_candidate(blurred, pool, state, "blur-%d" % win)
                blurred_r = np.convolve(blend_ri, kern, mode="same")
                consider_candidate(alpha * blurred + (1.0 - alpha) * blurred_r,
                                   pool, state, "blurmix-%d" % win)

    # Deterministic analytic families.
    param_deadline = min(deadline - 1200.0, start_time + 90.0)

    if time.monotonic() < param_deadline:
        a_vals = (0.0, 0.1, 0.2, 0.3, 0.4, 0.45, 0.49)
        b_vals = (-0.5, -0.25, -0.1, 0.0, 0.15, 0.35, 0.7, 1.2)
        tags = ("%d" % int(x * 100) for x in a_vals)
        for ai, av in enumerate(a_vals):
            for bi, bv in enumerate(b_vals):
                if time.monotonic() > param_deadline:
                    break
                s1 = gen_power_shape(n_main, av, bv)
                label = "pa%s-b%s" % (tags[ai], str(bv).replace(".", ""))
                consider_candidate(s1, pool, state, label)
                consider_candidate(s1[::-1], pool, state, label + "-r")

    if time.monotonic() < param_deadline:
        lams = (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0, 8.0)
        pows = (0.5, 1.0, 2.0)
        for lam in lams:
            for pw in pows:
                s1 = gen_exp_shape(n_main, lam, pw)
                consider_candidate(s1, pool, state, "el%dp%d" % (int(lam * 10), int(pw * 10)))
                consider_candidate(s1[::-1], pool, state, "el%dp%dr" % (int(lam * 10), int(pw * 10)))

    if time.monotonic() < param_deadline:
        for tilt in (-1.5, -1.0, -0.75, -0.5, -0.25, 0.0, 0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0, 1.5):
            t = (np.arange(n_main, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n_main)
            s1 = np.maximum(1.0 + tilt * t, 1e-5)
            tag = "lt%s" % str(tilt).replace(".", "").replace("-", "m")
            consider_candidate(s1, pool, state, tag)
            consider_candidate(s1[::-1], pool, state, tag + "r")

    if time.monotonic() < param_deadline:
        for amp in (-0.9, -0.6, -0.3, 0.0, 0.3, 0.6, 0.9):
            t = (np.arange(n_main, dtype=np.float64) + 0.5) / float(n_main)
            s1 = np.maximum(1.0 + amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * t), 1e-5)
            tag = "s1a%s" % str(amp).replace(".", "").replace("-", "m")
            consider_candidate(s1, pool, state, tag)
            s1b = np.maximum(1.0 + amp * np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * t), 1e-5)
            tag = "s2a%s" % str(amp).replace(".", "").replace("-", "m")
            consider_candidate(s1b, pool, state, tag)

    # Random analytic exploration.
    while time.monotonic() < param_deadline:
        kind = int(rng.integers(0, 5))
        s1 = gen_random_shape(rng, n_main, kind)
        consider_candidate(s1, pool, state, "rand-%d" % kind)

    if state["a"] is None:
        fallback = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64)
        consider_candidate(fallback, pool, state, "fallback-ones")

    if state["a"] is None:
        return [float(x) for x in np.full(1024, np.sqrt(2.0 * 1024.0) / 1024.0)]

    # Build a small pool of starting points for local optimization.
    starts = []
    starts.append((float(state["score"]), state["a"].copy(), state["label"] + "-best"))

    pool.sort(key=pool_key)
    added_labels = set([state["label"] + "-best"])
    for item in pool:
        if len(starts) >= 6:
            break
        label = item[2]
        if label in added_labels:
            continue
        added_labels.add(label)
        starts.append((float(item[0]), item[1].copy(), label))

    if given is not None and len(starts) < 8:
        starts.append((score_sequence(normalize_sum(given, np.sqrt(2.0 * given.size))),
                       given.copy(), "given-opt"))

    # Optimize sequential starts, allowing extra best-point polishing at the end.
    n_starts = len(starts)
    for si, item in enumerate(starts):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 240.0:
            break

        _, init_a, lbl = item
        remaining = deadline - time.monotonic() - 120.0
        if remaining < 120.0:
            break

        share = max(45.0, remaining / max(1, n_starts - si))
        budgets = (0.35, 0.35, 0.30)

        t0 = time.monotonic()
        end_f = min(deadline, t0 + budgets[0] * share)
        optimize_fista(init_a, state, deadline, rng, end_f)

        t0 = time.monotonic()
        end_a = min(deadline, t0 + budgets[1] * share)
        optimize_adam_z(init_a, state, deadline, rng, end_a)

        t0 = time.monotonic()
        end_s = min(deadline, t0 + budgets[2] * share)
        optimize_subgrad(init_a, state, deadline, rng, end_s)

    # Final polishing on the current best, using any leftover time.
    if state["a"] is not None:
        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 90.0:
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic() - 60.0
            if rem > 30.0:
                opt_end = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + 0.45 * rem)
                optimize_subgrad(state["a"], state, deadline, rng, opt_end)

        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 45.0:
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic() - 30.0
            if rem > 20.0:
                opt_end = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + 0.80 * rem)
                optimize_adam_z(state["a"], state, deadline, rng, opt_end)

        if time.monotonic() < deadline - 25.0:
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic() - 15.0
            if rem > 10.0:
                opt_end = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + 0.90 * rem)
                optimize_fista(state["a"], state, deadline, rng, opt_end)

    if state["a"] is None:
        final_arr = np.full(1024, np.sqrt(2.0 * 1024.0) / 1024.0, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        final_arr = np.asarray(state["a"], dtype=np.float64).copy()

    final_arr = np.nan_to_num(final_arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    final_arr = np.clip(final_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(final_arr.sum())
    if s < 0.01:
        final_arr = np.full(1024, np.sqrt(2.0 * 1024.0) / 1024.0, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        final_arr = final_arr * (np.sqrt(2.0 * float(final_arr.size)) / s)

    final_list = [float(x) for x in final_arr]
    print("Return: len=%d internal_best=%.8f" % (len(final_list), state["score"]))

    try:
        check = evaluate_sequence(final_list)
        print("evaluate_sequence direct check=%.8f" % check if np.isfinite(check) else
              "evaluate_sequence direct check=inf")
        if not np.isfinite(check):
            final_list = [float(x) for x in np.full(1024, np.sqrt(2.0 * 1024.0) / 1024.0)]
            print("Using safe fallback sequence.")
    except Exception:
        print("Final evaluation unavailable; returning best internal sequence.")

    return final_list
```
